34

**Layout**

Full-bleed black slide with a title block across the top spanning about 88% of the width, left-aligned at a margin of about 4.5% of the width. Below the title, two chart columns sit side by side on the left two-thirds (left column about 4.5% to 34%, right column about 38% to 68%), with a full-height photograph of the Sun bleeding off the right edge (65% to 100%). A logo sits bottom left, a source line runs along the bottom, and the page number sits bottom right.

**Visuals**

Chart 1 (left): vertical bar chart, untitled apart from its bold heading 'ELECTRICITY GENERATION HAS REMAINED LARGELY STAGNANT IN THE U.S. WHILE CHINA'S OUTPUT HAS GROWN STEADILY'. Three bars on a white baseline with eight thin white horizontal gridlines and no numeric axis. Bar 1 '08-'23 U.S.: value printed as <0.1%, shown as a very thin white sliver. Bar 2 '23-'25 U.S.: white bar, value printed as <3%. Bar 3 '23-'25 China: mid-grey #7F7F7F bar, value printed as ~ 6%, the tallest bar and the only one highlighted in grey. Category labels sit below the baseline; China's label is grey. No legend.
Chart 2 (right): stacked vertical bar chart, untitled apart from its bold heading 'US TERRESTRIAL COMPUTE SHORTFALL EXPECTED TO WIDEN FURTHER'. Single column with eight thin white horizontal gridlines and no numeric axis. Column total labelled '2025 DEMAND: 62 GW' above the column in grey. Lower segment grey #7F7F7F labelled '2025 SUPPLY: 49 GW' in white. Upper segment white labelled 'SUPPLY SHORTFALL' in grey. Segment heights are proportional to 49 and 13 GW, with the white shortfall segment about 21% of the column height. No legend.
Photograph: the Sun, a close-up of the solar surface in orange and yellow with bright active-region loops and dark sunspots, on the right edge of the slide. The disc is cropped by the right edge and the top-left of the image fades into black, so the photograph blends into the background with no visible boxed edge. Bright glowing prominences extend left from the limb. Lighting is self-emitted, with the brightest areas near the upper centre. Dominant colours are orange #E08A1E, yellow #F5C04A and black #000000.
Logo: small white SpaceX-style swoosh mark, bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with a full-bleed photograph of the Sun on the right. Black background with white and grey type. Title is set in a wide, regular-weight geometric sans with all caps, white #FFFFFF, roughly 7.6% of slide height per line, with about 9% line pitch. Chart headings are a bold condensed grotesque sans in all caps, white #FFFFFF, about 2.8% of slide height. Chart value and category labels are a regular-weight condensed sans, white #FFFFFF or grey #7F7F7F, about 2.3% of slide height. The large statistic 99.8% is a bold condensed sans in black #000000 over the sun image, about 7.6% of slide height, with its caption in bold condensed black at about 2.8%. Accents are grey #7F7F7F for the China bar and demand labels. Bars and gridlines are flat with no rounded corners, shadows or gradients; gridlines are thin white lines about 0.1% of slide height thick. Source line is regular sans in grey #A6A6A6, about 1.2% of slide height.

### Slide 35

**Text**

ORBITAL AI COMPUTE: READILY AVAILABLE, / CHEAPER, AND FASTER / READILY AVAILABLE / No current regulatory hurdles and environmentally friendly / Distribution enabled by Starlink technology / Power from solar energy: unlimited, clean, and lower cost / LOWER COST OF ONGOING OPERATIONS / Leverage radiative cooling architecture at lower cost relative to liquid or air cooling / Existing Starlink network enables cost efficient routing of data between compute clusters / and to end users on Earth / LOWER COST OF INITIAL DEPLOYMENT / Rocket reusability and high flight cadence reduces cost per kilogram to orbit / eventually to the cost of fuel / Advanced satellite manufacturing enables building of AI compute satellites at scale / and lower cost / FASTER TIME TO USEFUL TOKENS ON / NEW GENERATIONS OF COMPUTE / New generations of chips deliver step-function improvements in token efficiency / Deployment of new chips enabled by rapid cycles of payload delivery to orbit / 35

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark photograph with a large all-caps title at top left spanning two lines. Below it is a two-column layout: bold category labels in the left column (about x 4.5% to 37%) and regular bullet-like statements in the right column starting at x 41.3%. Four horizontal hairline dividers separate the groups, running from x 4.4% to 95.6%. The Starlink-style X logo sits bottom left and the slide number sits bottom right, both at about 4.5% from the left and right edges.

**Visuals**

A full-bleed photograph of a solar array in space: a diagonal grid of dark photovoltaic panels with thin light grid lines runs from lower left to upper right, a bright warm orange-white lens flare glows at the centre-right where the panel edges meet, and a faint blue-grey curve of Earth's limb appears at the right edge. Lighting is a warm orange point source against a near-black field. Dominant colours are black, charcoal, and amber-orange. The photo is uncropped at full bleed with no boxed frame, and a dark overlay darkens the left and top areas so the white text stays legible. Four thin grey horizontal rules are the only other visual elements; the X logo is a small white mark at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed black #000000 with a photograph of a solar array in space over it, darkened on the left. Black background (#000000) with the photograph behind everything. Title and labels in pure white #FFFFFF; body text in white #FFFFFF. Accent is the warm amber glow in the photo (about #E8A060). Title is a geometric grotesque sans-serif (GS Sans style), regular weight, all capitals, letter-spacing about 0.02em, about 8.3% of slide height at cap-height about 5.8%. Labels are a bold grotesque sans-serif, all caps, about 3.6% of slide height. Body is a regular grotesque sans-serif, about 2.4% of slide height, with generous spacing between items. Dividers are thin 0.75 pt lines in grey #8A8A8A. No corner radii, shadows or gradient shapes beyond the photo's own lighting.

**Review notes**

- Suggested repair: Fix legibility where text sits on photos or is very small, including slides 7, 31, 36, 42 and 43, by adding a dark panel or raising size and contrast.

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark slide with a single wide title across the top at roughly 7 to 15% of the height, spanning almost the full width with left and right margins of about 4.4%. Below it, a row of three labelled satellite/rocket columns on the left and right (Starship V3 at left, Starlink V3 beside it, AI Satellite at far right) with a centred three-column text grid (REDUCE & DELETE, KEEP, ADD) between them; a bottom centred two-line caption sits over the Earth limb, with a small footnote bottom-left and the slide number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Four images and one diagram-like arrangement. (1) Starship V3: a vertical silver-grey stainless steel launch vehicle, nose cone at top, shown full height as a near-vertical rendering, roughly 3.8% wide by 42% tall at x 8.8%, y 28.6%, lit from the upper left with specular highlights, on black, with a faint grey-metal gradient; not cropped. Below it a stat block reading 100 / Metric Tons / of Capacity / Per Launch / Over Time. (2) Starlink V3: a small dark grey vertical satellite bus with two rectangular solar-array panels and a small horizontal antenna/bar on its side, about 4.9% wide by 19% tall at x 20.7%, y 28.6%, with a tiny bar element at about x 22.5%, y 36.9%. (3) Horizontal arrow: a thin white line with an arrowhead at its right end, running from about x 30.7% to 81.4% at y 24.8%, behind the EVOLVING CONNECTIVITY TO COMPUTE label, which overlays it. (4) AI Satellite: a tall grey satellite with two long vertical solar panels and a central body with a small horizontal radiator/boom, about 4.9% wide by 42.4% tall at x 85.3%, y 28.6%, with a small bar at about x 83% to 88%, y 55%. (5) Earth photograph: a night-side view of the Earth's limb with a blue atmospheric glow along the horizon and city lights in dark continents, full-bleed across the bottom from about y 50.4% to 100%, darkened so the text stays legible. (6) Small X-shaped logo mark in white at bottom left, about x 9.6%, y 95.7%.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with a full-bleed Earth limb photograph across the bottom half. Black background (#000000) with a photographic Earth at the bottom. Titles and labels in white (#FFFFFF). Column headers REDUCE & DELETE in mid grey (#8C8C8C), KEEP and ADD in white (#FFFFFF). Items under REDUCE & DELETE in grey (#8C8C8C); other items in white (#FFFFFF). Typeface is a DIN-style geometric grotesque sans-serif (GSSans family), regular weight, all caps for the title and headers. Title approx 8% of slide height, body items approx 2.8%, column labels approx 3.2%, stat number approx 3.6%. Caption at bottom approx 3.6% regular caps white. Lines are thin white (#FFFFFF) with a small arrowhead; satellite and rocket graphics are greyscale with no rounded corners and no shadows.

**Layout**

Full-bleed black slide. Title block is left-aligned at about 4.4% from the left edge, starting at about 8% from the top: three-line uppercase headline in white, followed by two smaller subtitle lines in the same column. On the right, a closed loop diagram of eight labelled nodes sits in the upper-right and lower-centre area, with one rectangular box at top. Logo sits bottom-left and the slide number bottom-right, both near the margins.

**Visuals**

Diagram of white outlined boxes and white text nodes with white arrows. Top box 'SPACE AND CONNECTIVITY LEADERSHIP' (rectangle, thin white outline) has a straight downward arrow to the box 'INFRASTRUCTURE ADVANTAGE' (rectangle, thin white outline). From that box a curved arrow runs down-right to the node 'LARGER SCALE, LOWER COST PER TOKEN'. From that node one curved arrow runs down-left to 'MORE USERS SERVED' and one curved arrow runs down-right to 'IMPROVED QUALITY OF INTELLIGENCE'. 'MORE USERS SERVED' has a curved arrow down-right to 'BETTER USER EXPERIENCE AT SCALE'. 'IMPROVED QUALITY OF INTELLIGENCE' has a curved arrow down-left to 'BETTER USER EXPERIENCE AT SCALE'. 'BETTER USER EXPERIENCE AT SCALE' has a curved arrow down-left to 'GREATER USAGE, MONETIZATION'. 'GREATER USAGE, MONETIZATION' has a curved arrow up-left to 'REINVESTMENT TO SCALE COMPUTE INFRASTRUCTURE'. That node has a curved arrow up-right back to 'INFRASTRUCTURE ADVANTAGE', closing the loop. Background photograph: a large grey-brown cratered moon-like sphere occupies the lower-right, its limb arcing from about 27% x at the bottom to the top right, with a dark diagonal ring band crossing it from lower-left to upper-right and a small satellite silhouette near the lower centre. Lighting comes from the right, so the right side is bright grey and the left side is dark blue-black. A small photograph of Earth, blue oceans and white clouds, lit from the upper left with a faint glow, sits at the left at mid-height, unboxed and not cropped. The logo is a small white stylised X mark.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 covering the whole slide, with the moon photograph and Earth photograph placed on top. Black background #000000 throughout. Headline and node text in white #FFFFFF; subtitle lines white #FFFFFF; diagram lines and box outlines white #FFFFFF at thin stroke (about 1 pt). Headline is a light-weight geometric sans-serif in uppercase with wide letter-spacing, similar to DIN, cap height about 5.3% of slide height. Subtitles are the same light sans in uppercase, slightly smaller. Diagram node text is a bold condensed-leaning uppercase sans-serif similar to DIN Bold, about 2.8% of slide height. Arrows are white thin lines with small solid triangular arrowheads. Box corners are square. No shadows or gradients on text or shapes. Photographs are unframed and blend into the black.

**Layout**

A single-column title across the top left at about 4 percent margin, with a phone mockup in the left third below it and three statements stacked in the right half, left-aligned to a common edge at about 28 percent of the width. The footer carries a logo at bottom left, a note beside it, and the page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

A photographic phone mockup in the left third showing a Grok app screen in a dark rounded phone frame. The screen shows a status bar reading 9:41, a hamburger icon, an Ask and Imagine segmented toggle, a compose icon, a user bubble reading What is Terafab, an answer with a bold Terafab lead, a photo of an aerial view of a factory with TESLA and SPACEX signs on the roof and overlaid stats 0.5 TW and 1 TW/year, a pixelated dark magenta and teal image with the TERAFAB wordmark, and a bottom input bar with an attachment icon, an Expert pill and a white Speak pill. The factory photo is warm sunlit with orange-brown tones, boxed inside the screen. No charts or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Pure black background #000000 with white #FFFFFF titles and body text and no accent colour. The title is set in a light geometric sans-serif (GS Sans style) in all capitals with wide letter-spacing, about 7.6 percent of slide height. **Layout**

Three-column layout on a full black slide with a single-line title across the top spanning nearly the full width, starting at the left margin of about 5% and ending near the right edge. Column headers sit at the same height at the top of each column, with the left column holding Selling Compute (Anthropic and Google agreements, centred), the middle column holding Selling Intelligence: Enterprise (Grok and Cursor, centred), and the right column holding Selling Intelligence: Consumer (Grok and three metrics, left-aligned beside numbers). Two thin vertical grey rules separate the columns; a short horizontal rule divides the Anthropic and Google blocks in the left column. Source and note lines sit at bottom left beside a small X logo, and the page number sits at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Two Grok logos (white stylised circular-slash symbol with the wordmark Grok in bold white sans-serif), one in the middle column centred at about x 44.8-57.1%, y 38.7-46.9%, and one in the right column at about x 76.5-88.8%, y 38.7-46.9%. One Cursor logo in the middle column at about x 44.0-57.8%, y 62.2-68.0%: a dark grey (#4A4A4A to #8A8A8A) faceted cube-like hexagon icon with a white cursor arrow shape, followed by the wordmark CURSOR in bold white geometric sans-serif capitals. Metrics in the right column are large white numerals with small grey-white labels. Two vertical hairline dividers in grey #7A7A7A, about 0.15% wide, spanning y 21.0-90.3% at x about 34.8% and 67.0%. A horizontal hairline divider in grey #7A7A7A at about y 59.4%, x 5.9-31.5%. Small white X logo (stylised X with a swept stroke) at bottom left, about x 4.4-8.0%, y 94.4-97.1%. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 fill covering the whole slide. Solid black background #000000 with no gradient or texture. Title and column headers are white #FFFFFF; body text is white #FFFFFF or near-white #E8E8E8; footnote and page number are light grey #B0B0B0; accent colour is none beyond the white logos and grey hairlines #7A7A7A. Title is set in a light-weight geometric sans-serif in all capitals with slight letter-spacing, about 8.3% of slide height (cap height about 5.8%). Column headers are bold sans-serif all capitals, about 3.8% of slide height. Sub-heads such as Anthropic and Google are regular sans-serif, about 3.3%. Body text is regular GS Sans-style sans-serif, about 2.5% of slide height. Large figures such as $1.25B and ~117M are regular sans-serif at about 6.3% to 6.7%. Footnote text is about 1.3% of slide height. Lines are hairline with no shadows, corner radii or rounded shapes.

Near the right end, two copper-coloured flat flaps or fins stand out, one at the top right angled upward and one larger rectangular panel hanging downward toward the lower centre, with small hinge and bracket hardware visible. The lower half of the body is lit by a warm copper-to-peach glow, and the upper body is lit with cool grey highlights on the carbon texture. Composition is horizontal, with the object spanning most of the width and the left-centre area showing black space behind the title. Lighting is directional and warm from below, with soft reflections. Dominant colours are black #000000, copper #B8704A, and peach #E9B593, with grey highlights on the upper body. The photo is not cropped and is full-bleed, with no overlay or tint. A faint darker haze is visible at the bottom-right corner.

**Text**

OUR GROWTH STRATEGY
SPACE
Increase launch payload capacity
Establish the lunar economy, including cargo transport, manufacturing, and energy production on the Moon
CONNECTIVITY
Grow Starlink Broadband consumers
Grow Starlink Broadband enterprise and government customers
Expand our Starlink Mobile offering
Increase the capacity of our constellations
AI
Grow consumer AI platform monetization
Grow X monetization
Deepen enterprise and government adoption
Increase the scale of our AI compute infrastructure
Deploy orbital AI compute at scale
Monetize across selling compute and selling intelligence
Design and manufacture our own chips
Launch digital human augmentation
42

**Layout**

Full-bleed photograph with no grid or boxes. The title sits top-left at about 5% from the left edge and 7% from the top. Three text columns sit over the lower and middle sky: SPACE at left (x about 18%), CONNECTIVITY in the centre (x about 44%), and AI at right (x about 73%), each with a short vertical rule above its heading and items stacked left-aligned beneath it. A small X logo sits bottom-left and the page number sits bottom-right.

**Visuals**

One full-bleed night-to-dusk photograph of a rocket launch seen from a hilltop. A bright orange-white exhaust trail rises diagonally from the bottom-left horizon (about x 7%, y 88%) in a long arc to the upper-right corner (about x 90%, y 10%), fading to a faint violet streak at its tip. A few faint star streaks and one small bright white point sit in the dark sky. The horizon is a low band of misty hill silhouettes in dark grey-brown with a sparse town of warm city lights at bottom-right. Lighting is the glowing trail against a navy-to-orange gradient sky. Composition is wide landscape, with the trail as the main diagonal leading from the lower left to the upper right. The photo is uncropped and covers the whole slide, with no overlay or tint. Dominant colours are deep navy #0E1430, violet #3A3560, orange #E8843A and warm white #FFF3E0. A logo sits bottom-left, the X mark in white, and the slide number sits bottom-right.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed night-sky photograph: navy #0E1430 at top fading to violet #3A3560 and orange #E8843A at the horizon, over dark hills #1A1520 at the bottom. Photographic background with no solid colour panel. Title is white #FFFFFF, regular-weight geometric sans (GSSans), all caps, slightly open letter-spacing, about 7.9% of slide height. Column headings SPACE, CONNECTIVITY and AI are white #FFFFFF in semi-bold GSSans, all caps for the first two and mixed case for AI, about 2.2% of slide height. Body items are white #F2F2F2 regular GSSans at about 1.9% of slide height with generous spacing between items. Thin white vertical rules, about 0.1% wide, sit above each heading. Accent colour is the orange glow #F5A050 of the trail. No shapes, corner radii or shadows beyond the photo itself.

**Review notes**

- Review: Weak spots are some text over photos with low contrast (slides 7, 31, 42, 43), a few tiny captions and labels (slides 6, 27, 36, 52 to 54), and a few busy slides (9, 29, 36).

**Text**

PIONEERING THE LUNAR ECONOMY
HUMAN MOON RETURN
Land humans on the moon by late 2020s for the first / time since 1972 with NASA's Artemis
Use Starship for transport
Establish sustainable lunar presence for science, / exploration, and industrialization
ESTABLISHING A LUNAR BASE
Prove systems, habitats, and Starship
Act as test-case for resource sustainability / necessary for human survival beyond Earth
Launch satellites into orbit / deep space
BUILDING GROUND FOR AI / COMPUTE SATELLITES
Establish lunar factories to manufacture AI satellites
Harness solar power and lunar mass driver
Grow AI compute to terawatts annually
43

**Layout**

Full-bleed photographic background with the title in the top-left band over the dark sky, and three equal text columns in the lower half, starting at about 5 percent, 38 percent and 68 percent of the width; each column has a bold uppercase heading followed by three bullet-style lines separated by hairline rules. Thin vertical hairlines divide the columns, the SpaceX-style logo sits bottom-left and the page number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

One photorealistic CGI render of a lunar settlement, full-bleed and uncropped, seen from an elevated oblique viewpoint looking across the grey regolith. A dense cluster of white domed habitats, white-lit structures and small vehicles sits at the centre-top, with dark solar panel arrays forming a long diagonal strip running from centre to lower right and a dark solar field to the upper left. Two landing pads with angular flat-topped roofs stand in the lower left and centre, a third pad sits at the right edge, and a row of tall white antennas and masts runs along the left and right horizon. A small white rocket is visible in the black sky just right of the title's 'PIONEERING'. Lighting is diffuse sunlight with soft shadows; dominant colours are grey, charcoal and black with white highlights. No overlay or tint beyond the natural dark sky at the top.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with a full-bleed photograph of the lunar base render covering the whole slide. Black background (#000000) behind a full-bleed grey CGI render. Title and headings are white (#FFFFFF); bullet text is white (#FFFFFF) at regular weight; hairline rules and dividers are light grey-white (#D0D0D0) at about 0.1 percent of slide height. Typeface is a geometric grotesque sans-serif, DIN-like: the title is regular weight, uppercase, with slight letter-spacing; column headings are bold uppercase; bullet text is regular weight in sentence case. Title cap height is about 6 percent of slide height (font size about 8 percent); headings about 3.5 percent; body text about 2.5 percent. Lines are square-cornered hairlines; no shapes, shadows or gradients.

### Slide 44

**Text**

WELL-POSITIONED TO CREATE NEW MARKETS
WITH MULTI-TRILLION-DOLLAR ECONOMIC
OPPORTUNITIES
POINT-TO-POINT TERRESTRIAL TRAVEL
IN-ORBIT MANUFACTURING
ENERGY PRODUCTION AND
MANUFACTURING ON MARS
PASSENGER AND CARGO TRANSPORT TO MARS
ASTEROID MINING
LUNAR MANUFACTURING AND TRANSPORT
44

**Layout**

A three-column by two-row grid of six equal-sized photographs, each with a centred all-caps caption beneath it, sits below a large left-aligned three-line title that spans most of the slide width at the top. The SpaceX-style logo sits at bottom-left and the page number at bottom-right, with about 5% side margins and a black field throughout.

**Visuals**

Six photographic renderings in a 3x2 grid, all rectangular, boxed, not cropped to a shape, with no overlays or tints. Row 1 left, 'Point-to-point terrestrial travel': a silver stainless-steel rocket-ship (Starship-type) with black heat-shield tile patches and angled fins, angled diagonally from lower-left to upper-right through a white-grey cloud sky; soft diffuse daylight, dominant colours silver, grey and white. Row 1 centre, 'In-orbit manufacturing': a satellite-like orbital platform with a dark hexagonal lattice body and two long dark solar arrays, above a blue-white Earth limb against black space; a small spacecraft at upper right and a small grey sphere at lower left; lit from the sunlit Earth side, dominant colours black, blue and white. Row 1 right, 'Energy production and manufacturing on Mars': a rust-orange Martian landscape with a tall white Starship on a launch stand at left, a small figure in a white suit near centre, a wheeled trailer carrying cylindrical silver processing tanks at right, and a foreground figure in a dark jacket at left edge; warm dusty daylight, dominant colours orange, rust and silver. Row 2 left, 'Passenger and cargo transport to Mars': interior of a spacecraft cabin with a white curved seat in the foreground, dark grey walls, and a wall screen at right showing the planet Mars in red-orange; cool grey and white with orange accent, low interior lighting. Row 2 centre, 'Asteroid mining': a dark silver cylindrical spacecraft with fins and a bright nozzle end, floating in black space beside a grey rocky asteroid surface at right; dominant colours black, grey and silver. Row 2 right, 'Lunar manufacturing and transport': a long white elevated tube-like transport structure on thin angled pylons running diagonally across grey lunar regolith under a dark starry sky, with a bright light band along the tube; dominant colours black, grey and white.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Solid black background with white all-caps sans-serif type throughout, using GS Sans Regular (geometric grotesque) with slight letter-spacing. Title is large, white #FFFFFF, roughly 7.6% of slide height per line with generous line spacing. Captions are small white #FFFFFF in the same family at roughly 1.8% of slide height. No accent colour; the photographs supply the colour. Photos have square corners, no borders, no shadows, no gradients.

### Slide 45

**Text**

TRACK RECORD OF / CAPITAL ALLOCATION / AND VALUE CREATION

**Layout**

A single full-bleed photograph fills the slide. The title sits in three left-aligned lines in the left-middle third, starting at about 4.4% from the left edge. The SpaceX logo sits alone in the bottom-left corner at about 4.4% from the left and 94% from the top. There is no footer and no visible slide number.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph of the planet Mars seen from orbit, with a stainless-steel Starship vehicle in flight. The planet's curved limb runs from the upper-left to the right edge, with a thin blue atmospheric line above it. The planet surface is rust-brown with eroded ridges and craters, and the sky is near-black. The Starship is shown nose-up and angled from upper-left to lower-right, at about 50% to 74% of the slide width and 9% to 81% of its height. Its body is polished silver-grey with panel seams, the nose is dark, and the fins are on the upper and lower right. A bright teal-cyan glow and plume trail behind and along its left side, extending toward the upper-left. Lighting comes from the teal engine glow and the sunlit planet. Dominant colours are rust-brown #8B3A24, near-black #0D0D0D, teal #3FD0D0 and steel grey #9AA0A6. The photograph is not cropped, not boxed and has no tint or overlay apart from the glow.

**Design**

Background is a full-bleed photograph over a #0D0D0D fill. Title is white #FFFFFF uppercase GSSans-Regular, a geometric sans-serif with regular weight and slight letter spacing. Title cap height is about 5.3% of slide height, so font size is about 7.6% of slide height, with line pitch about 8.7%. There is no body text. The logo is white. No shapes, rules, corner radii or shadows are present on the slide.

**Layout**

Two-line all-caps title sits top-left at the left margin (about 4.4% from the left edge) across the top of the slide. Below it a central bar chart is framed by two white chevron arrows: a left callout block ($9B) and a right callout block (Building The Infrastructure of the Future). A borderless three-row-area table ('WHILE FUNDING R&D INVESTMENT') sits along the bottom with a thin divider rule, followed by a small footnote and logo at the bottom left and the page number at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Bar chart titled SPACE AND CONNECTIVITY SEGMENT ADJ. EBITDA ($B), vertical bars, all white (#FFFFFF) on black, no y-axis, no gridlines, thin white horizontal baseline. Three bars: 2023 labelled $2.6 (about 2.6 on the scale, bar height about 17% of plot height), 2024 labelled $5.0 (bar about 32% of plot height), 2025 labelled $7.8 (bar about 50% of plot height and highlighted as the tallest). Category labels under the bars: 2023, 2024, 2025. No legend, no axis ranges printed. Two white chevron/triangle arrows pointing right: one left of the chart (x 15.8 to 20.2%) and one right of the chart (x 76.3 to 80.7%), each about 4.4% wide and 35.2% tall, solid white with no outline. Table: columns with row labels Space and Connectivity (bold), values for 2023, 2024, 2025 columns: Space $1.5, $1.8, $3.0; Connectivity $0.4, $0.5, $0.6; a right-hand column labelled Starship Launch Infrastructure (Space row) and Broadband & Mobile Constellation (Connectivity row). Thin white rule separates the two rows. Small white SpaceX-style swoosh logo bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 across the full slide. Black background (#000000) with white (#FFFFFF) titles, body text, chart bars and arrows; no accent colour. Title in a regular-weight geometric grotesque sans-serif (GSSans style), all caps, slight letter-spacing, roughly 7.5% of slide height per line. Chart heading and table heading in a bold condensed sans (DIN-like), all caps, about 2.2% of slide height. Big callout $9B in regular sans about 6% of slide height. Labels and values in regular sans about 2.2% of slide height. Footnote about 1.1% of slide height. Bars and arrows are flat solid white with sharp corners, no shadows, no gradients. Thin white divider line about 0.1% thick.

### Slide 47

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Three-year revenue and adjusted EBITDA bars for each segment, with growth labels, let the viewer compare business units directly.
- Review: Charts carry the argument directly, with headline claims as titles and big numerals (slides 13, 24, 29, 47 to 54).
- What would change the score: I would thin the smaller gray captions and the run of near-identical reconciliation tables, and add a clear takeaway line to the financial slides 47 to 54.

**Text**

SIGNIFICANT GROWTH AND SCALE ENABLES / INVESTMENT IN VALUE CREATION / REVENUE ($B) / 2025 / Growth / 33% / $18.7 / 22% / $14.0 / $10.4 / 50% / 8% / 2023 / 2024 / 2025 / ADJ. EBITDA ($B) / $6.6 / $5.4 / $3.8 / 2023 / 2024 / 2025 / SPACE / CONNECTIVITY / AI / Note: Segment Adjusted EBITDA is a non-GAAP measure. Please see Appendix for reconciliation to most comparable GAAP measure. / 47

**Layout**

Two side-by-side bar charts fill the lower two-thirds of a black slide, with a two-line all-caps title across the top-left and a shared legend centred below the charts. Title starts at about 4.4% from the left edge and sits above both charts; chart headings REVENUE ($B) (left) and ADJ. EBITDA ($B) (right) sit on one row at about 25% height, left-aligned to their chart areas. A small footnote with a logo sits bottom-left at about 94.5% height and the page number sits bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Chart 1, stacked column chart, titled REVENUE ($B), three columns for 2023, 2024 and 2025 with total labels $10.4, $14.0 and $18.7 above them (no axis values printed, no gridlines, baseline only). Series stacked bottom to top: SPACE grey #ACACAC, CONNECTIVITY light blue #A9D3FD, AI blue #0089E8. Right of the 2025 column are printed percentage annotations: 2025 Growth 33% (white, 33% bold italic), 22% in blue italic beside the AI segment, 50% in light blue italic beside the connectivity segment, and 8% in grey italic beside the space segment. Segment heights are not labelled; about values from the image: 2023 space about 35%, connectivity about 20%, AI about 15% of column height; 2025 space about 25% of column height. Chart 2, stacked column chart, titled ADJ. EBITDA ($B), columns for 2023, 2024 and 2025 with total labels $3.8, $5.4 and $6.6. Stacks bottom to top: SPACE grey, CONNECTIVITY light blue, AI blue. The 2025 column has a grey space segment above the baseline and a blue AI segment below the zero baseline (a negative value), with a light blue connectivity segment above; no axis values printed. Legend centred below both charts: grey square SPACE, light blue square CONNECTIVITY, blue square AI. Baseline is a thin light grey horizontal line under each chart. The X logo is a white stylised X with a swoosh, at bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 covering the whole slide. Solid black background with white titles and labels, light blue and blue data accents. Title is in an all-caps geometric sans, regular weight, white #FFFFFF, two lines, cap height about 8% of slide height. Chart headings and data labels in the same sans, regular weight, white #FFFFFF, about 2.3% of slide height. Percentage annotations are italic. Blue accent #0089E8, light blue #A9D3FD, grey #ACACAC. Bars are flat rectangles with square corners and no shadows, outlines or gradients.

**Layout**

Single-column title band across the top with the title flush left at about 4.4% from the left edge. Below it, a bar chart fills the left roughly 68% of the slide, and a narrow vertical divider at about 70% separates it from a right-hand text column of bold lead-in and three statements separated by thin rules. A small logo sits bottom left and the page number bottom right, both about 4% from the edges.

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked vertical bar chart titled CAPITAL EXPENDITURES ($B). Three bars for categories 2023, 2024 and 2025, with no y-axis and no gridlines. Baseline is a thin white horizontal axis line from about 5% to 68% of slide width at about 82% of slide height. Series are SPACE (grey #ADADAD, bottom), CONNECTIVITY (light blue #AAD4FE, middle) and AI (blue #008AE9, top). Printed totals above each bar: 2023 $4.4, 2024 $11.2, 2025 $20.7. Segment values are not printed; estimates: 2023 SPACE about $1.5B, CONNECTIVITY about $2.5B, AI about $0.4B; 2024 SPACE about $2.1B, CONNECTIVITY about $3.5B, AI about $5.6B; 2025 SPACE about $3.8B, CONNECTIVITY about $4.2B, AI about $12.7B. Legend centred below the bars: grey square SPACE, light blue square CONNECTIVITY, blue square AI. No points highlighted. Also a thin grey vertical divider line and two thin grey horizontal rules in the right column. Logo: a small white stylised X-and-swoosh mark, bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Full black background with no gradient or texture. Title in white (#FFFFFF) regular-weight all-caps grotesque sans-serif resembling GS Sans, about 8 percent of slide height. Subtitle and legend in white, all-caps, regular weight, about 3.5 and 2.6 percent of slide height. Right-column bold lead-in in white semibold sans-serif, body in white regular sans-serif at about 2.1 percent of slide height. Accents: blue #008AE9 for AI, light blue #AAD4FE for CONNECTIVITY, grey #ADADAD for SPACE. Divider and rules in mid grey #6E6E6E, about 0.1 percent of slide height thick. Chart shapes are flat rectangles with square corners and no shadows, no outlines.

**Layout**

A full-width three-row comparison table below a single-line-broken title, with the title flush left at about 4.4% margin from the left edge. No photograph, chart or diagram. A table with four columns and three data rows plus a header row. Header row: 2025 (column 2) and Future Target (column 3), in bold grey. Row 2: Gross Margin (%) | 49% | ~70% | Expansion as AI monetization grows and Connectivity margins expand with next generation satellites. Two full-width thin light-grey horizontal rules separate the rows. One small white logo mark (a stylised swept X-like mark) sits bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Pure black background (#000000) with no gradient, texture or imagery. Title in white (#FFFFFF) regular-weight all-caps geometric grotesque sans-serif (GS Sans style), about 7.5% of slide height per line with generous line spacing. Column header text bold grey (#A6A6A6). Row labels bold white (#FFFFFF) about 4% of slide height. Numeric values regular white about 4.2% of slide height. Commentary text regular white about 3% of slide height with about 1.2 line spacing. Footnote text regular light grey (#CCCCCC) about 1% of slide height. Divider rules are thin light grey (#D9D9D9), about 0.1% of slide height, no rounded corners, no shadows.

**Layout**

Photograph, full-bleed, uncropped at edges. Subject: a spacecraft cabin interior. On the left, a white curved helmet-like headrest shell and a black leather-textured seat back in the foreground. Behind them are three arched dark windows along the top-left and a dark curved bulkhead. On the right, a large dark wall-mounted display shows a glowing orange Mars globe with a thin cyan-blue and red elliptical orbit path, plus a dark UI with a left sidebar, a right panel with a stacked list of timeline items, and a bottom row of four small cyan-lit buttons. Through the arched window at right, a reddish Mars surface is visible. Lighting: low-key, warm orange light from the Mars view and cool grey highlights on the white shell. Dominant colours: near-black #0A0A0C, warm rust orange #C0572B, off-white #E8E8E6, cyan accent #3FD0E0. Framing: medium close-up, slightly angled from the left, shallow depth of field with the foreground blurred at the bottom. No overlay or tint beyond natural vignetting. Small white SpaceX-style swoosh logo at bottom-left.

Title text is white #FFFFFF, uppercase, in a light geometric sans-serif (GSSans Regular style), about 7.5% of slide height, thin to regular weight with slight letter spacing. No body text. **Layout**

Full-bleed black slide with a two-column arrangement: the left column (about 4% to 67% of the width) holds the title and two stacked bar charts, the right column (about 72% to 97%) holds four text blocks separated by thin horizontal rules, with a vertical divider between columns at about 70% of the width. The title sits top-left spanning two lines, left-aligned to a margin of about 4.4%. A logo sits bottom-left with the footnote beside it, and the page number sits bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Two vertical bar charts stacked in the left column, both with plain grey bars and no legend and no highlighted points. Chart 1 is titled REVENUE ($B) and shows revenue bars for 2023 ($3.6B), 2024 ($3.8B) and 2025 ($4.1B), with a YoY (%) row beneath the year labels showing 2023 blank, 2024 7% and 2025 8%. Chart 2 is titled ADJ. EBITDA ($B) and shows bars for 2023 ($1.0B), 2024 ($1.2B) and 2025 ($0.7B), with an R&D ($B) row beneath showing 2023 $1.5, 2024 $1.8 and 2025 $3.0. Each chart has a single thin light-grey baseline and no visible value axis or gridlines. Bars are flat #ACACAC rectangles with no outlines. Values are printed above each bar. The logo at bottom-left is the white SpaceX-style mark, a stylised X with a swoosh. Nothing is cropped or photographed.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 fill covering the whole slide. Pure black background, white text throughout, with grey #ACACAC used for the chart bars and mid-grey #5A5A5A for the divider and rules. Title and body text are white #FFFFFF. The typeface is GSSans-Regular, a clean geometric grotesque sans-serif in regular weight, with the title set in capitals. Title cap height is about 5.5% of slide height; right column body text is about 2.3% of slide height; chart labels about 2.1%; footnote and page number about 1.1%. Shapes are flat with square corners, no shadows, no gradients.

**Layout**

Two-zone layout on a black field: a large title block across the top spanning roughly the left 85% of the width, a left-hand chart area with two stacked column charts (Revenue above, Adj. EBITDA below) occupying about x 4.6 to 68 percent, and a narrow right-hand text column starting at x 72.4 percent separated from the charts by a thin vertical rule at x 70.1 percent. The title is top-left with left margin about 4.4 percent; the footnote and logo sit along the bottom at about y 95 percent, and the page number sits bottom-right at x 97 percent.

**Visuals**

Chart 1, Revenue stacked column chart, no printed chart title beyond the label REVENUE ($B) above it. Three stacked columns for 2023, 2024 and 2025, with x-axis labels 2023, 2024, 2025 and no y-axis, gridlines or axis ticks. Each column is totalled above by a printed value: $3.9 (2023), $7.6 (2024), $11.4 (2025). Series 1 Consumer, light blue #A9D3FD, at the base of each column. Series 2 Enterprise & Govt., grey #9B9B9B, stacked on top. Segment values are not printed; estimated segment heights: 2023 consumer about $2.7B and enterprise about $1.2B; 2024 consumer about $5.3B and enterprise about $2.3B; 2025 consumer about $8.1B and enterprise about $3.3B. Legend at the left of the chart: Enterprise & Govt. in white and Consumer in light blue #A9D3FD, stacked, aligned to the grey and blue segments. Beneath the x-axis a row labelled YoY (%) prints 96% under 2024 and 50% under 2025; no value under 2023. No highlighted points. Chart 2, Adj. EBITDA column chart, label ADJ. EBITDA ($B) above it. Also: a thin white horizontal baseline under each chart, a thin grey vertical divider, and three thin grey horizontal rules in the right column.

### Slide 54

**Review notes**

- Review: Charts carry the argument directly, with headline claims as titles and big numerals (slides 13, 24, 29, 47 to 54).
- Review: Weak spots are some text over photos with low contrast (slides 7, 31, 42, 43), a few tiny captions and labels (slides 6, 27, 36, 52 to 54), and a few busy slides (9, 29, 36).
- What would change the score: I would thin the smaller gray captions and the run of near-identical reconciliation tables, and add a clear takeaway line to the financial slides 47 to 54.

54

**Layout**

Title runs across the top-left at about 4% margin from the left edge, with no subtitle. Two stacked bar charts occupy the left about 70% of the width, with a vertical grey rule at about 70% x separating them from a right-hand column of four text items divided by thin horizontal rules; a footnote and a small logo sit along the bottom, and the page number is at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Two bar charts. Chart 1, top, titled REVENUE ($B): stacked columns for 2023, 2024 and 2025 on a shared baseline with no y-axis, ticks or range printed. Bottom segment is Ads in blue #0089E8; top segment is AI Solutions + Infrastructure in grey #9B9B9B. Totals printed above bars: 2023 $3.0, 2024 $2.6, 2025 $3.2. Segment values are not printed; about $2.3B Ads and about $0.7B AI Solutions in 2023, about $1.7B Ads and about $0.9B AI Solutions in 2024, about $1.9B Ads and about $1.3B AI Solutions in 2025. Below the x-axis years is a YoY (%) row: 2024 (12)%, 2025 22%, with no 2023 value printed. Legend is the two labels at left: 'AI Solutions + Infrastructure' beside the grey and 'Ads' beside the blue. Chart 2, bottom, titled ADJ. EBITDA ($B): columns on a baseline with no y-axis, ticks or range printed; 2023 blue column labelled $1.2 above, 2024 blue column labelled $0.3 above, 2025 blue column below the baseline labelled ($1.2). Years 2023, 2024, 2025 under the axis. No highlighted points. Dashed grey vertical divider lines sit between 2024 and 2025 in both charts and a short dashed line marks the footnote, all at thin stroke. Horizontal baselines are thin light grey lines spanning both charts.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 covering the whole slide. Black background #000000 with white #FFFFFF text throughout. Title is a regular-weight sans-serif in all caps (GS Sans-like, close to Helvetica), white #FFFFFF, about 7.8% of slide height. Chart labels and body text are regular-weight sans-serif, white #FFFFFF, about 2.0% to 2.6% of slide height. Accent colours: Ads blue #0089E8 and grey #9B9B9B for the AI segment. Vertical rule and horizontal dividers are thin grey #7A7A7A, about 0.2% stroke. Dashed dividers are grey #9B9B9B. No corner radii, shadows or gradients.

### Slide 55

**Text**

OUR KEY METRICS / SPACE / MASS TO ORBIT / Total kilograms of payload deployed to orbit in a period, calculated as the sum of verified mass (including Starlink satellites, customer payloads, and development cargo) from all successful orbital and flight tests / LAUNCHES / Total number of successful orbital and flight tests across rockets in a period, including Starlink deployments, development tests, and third-party customer launches and excluding any cancellations or scrubs / CONNECTIVITY / STARLINK SUBSCRIBERS / Unique Service Lines assigned to Starlink.com accounts that do not have direct, negotiated agreements with the Starlink sales team / STARLINK ARPU / Service revenue generated from Starlink Subscribers divided by the average number of Starlink Subscribers during a period and by the number of months in a period / AI / NAMEPLATE COMPUTE DRAW / Total number of GPUs installed in the data centers at the end of a period multiplied by the respective all- / in power draw, reflecting installed capacity and not actual power consumption or utilization / ALL SEGMENTS / SEGMENT INCOME (LOSS) / FROM OPERATIONS / Income (loss) from operations for each segment in a period / SEGMENT ADJ. EBITDA / Segment income (loss) from operations excluding depreciation and amortization, share-based compensation, restructuring charges, and impairment in a period / SEGMENT CAPEX / Capital expenditure incurred by each segment in a period / 55

**Layout**

Full-bleed black slide with a single left-aligned title at the top left (about 4.4% from the left edge, 7% from the top) and a two-column table below: a narrow left column of four stacked category blocks (x 4.4% to 18.7%) and a wide right column of label/description rows (labels at x 21%, descriptions at x 41.4%) separated by thin horizontal rules spanning x 20.3% to 96.9%. The logo sits bottom left at about x 4.4%, y 94.5%, and the page number bottom right at about x 96.5%, y 95.5%; there is no footer text.

**Visuals**

None. The slide contains no charts, photographs, icons or illustrations. Its only graphic elements are the four dark grey category blocks, seven thin horizontal divider rules and the small white logo mark at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 across the whole slide. Black background (#000000) with white (#FFFFFF) title, labels and descriptions and no accent colour. Title is a regular-weight, all-capitals sans-serif in the GS Sans family, about 7.6% of slide height in cap height terms (em about 7.6%). Row labels are bold all-caps sans-serif (closest common: Helvetica Bold or Arial Bold), about 2.8% em. Descriptions are regular-weight sans-serif in sentence case, about 2.3% em, with tight line spacing of about 2.7% of slide height. Category blocks are flat #171717 rectangles with square corners, no shadow and no gradient; category labels are bold white all-caps sans-serif about 3.6% em, centred. Divider rules are 1 px dark grey (#333333) lines. No shadows or gradients anywhere.

**Layout**

A single-column slide with a two-line all-caps title at the top left, a full-width financial table occupying the middle and lower-middle area, and a small footnote with a logo at the bottom left and the page number at the bottom right. Left margin is about 4.5% of slide width; the table spans about 4.6% to 95.5% horizontally, with the title and table left-aligned on the same margin. The table has no vertical gridlines; the column gap between the 2025 and Q1 2026 columns is a visible break in the rules.

**Visuals**

Table with 5 columns and 12 rows (plus header). Header row: first cell '(in billions)' in italic, then columns '2023', '2024', '2025', 'Q1 2026' in bold. Row 'Net income (loss)' in bold: ($4.6), $0.8, ($4.9), ($4.3). Row 'Add (deduct):' with no values. Indented rows: Depreciation and amortization $2.6, $3.8, $6.7, $2.4; Share-based compensation $0.7, $0.8, $1.9, $0.6; Restructuring charges $0.2, $0.2, $0.5, ($0.0); Impairments $3.8, $0.1, $0.0, blank; Interest expense $1.7, $1.6, $1.9, $0.7; Interest income ($0.2), ($0.4), ($0.5), ($0.2); Other (income) expense, net $0.0, ($1.0), $0.2, $1.9; Provision for income taxes ($0.4), ($0.5), $0.7, $0.0. Thin white horizontal rules sit under the header row, above the Adjusted EBITDA row, and below the Adjusted EBITDA row; the rules are broken between the 2025 and Q1 2026 columns. No chart, photo or highlighted points. Logo: small white stylised X mark at bottom left.

Background: Solid black #000000. Pure black background with white text throughout, no accent colour. The footnote and logo sit bottom left, and the page number sits bottom right, both at the margins.

One table, no chart, photograph or diagram. Columns: row label, 2023, 2024, 2025, then a gap, then Q1 2026. **Layout**

Single-column layout on a full black slide. The two-line all-caps title sits top-left at the left margin (about 4.4% from the left edge), spanning roughly the top fifth of the slide. A bold section label 'AI' sits left-aligned below the title, followed by a full-width financial table left-aligned at the same margin, ending about two-thirds down the slide with empty space below. The footer has a small logo at bottom-left, a note line beside it, and the slide number at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Table, no chart. Columns: row label column, then 2023, 2024, 2025 and Q1 2026 (values centred under each heading, with a wider gap before Q1 2026). Units: billions of dollars, stated as '(in billions)' in the header row. Rows: Income (loss) from operations (bold): ($4.0), ($1.6), ($6.4), ($2.5). Add (deduct): row with no values. Depreciation and amortization: $1.2, $1.7, $3.6, $1.5. Share-based compensation: $0.0, $0.0, $1.1, $0.4. Restructuring charges: $0.2, $0.2, $0.5, ($0.0). Impairment: $3.8, -, -, -. Segment Adjusted EBITDA (bold): $1.2, $0.3, ($1.2), ($0.6). Lines: a white rule under the header row across the 2023 to 2025 columns and a separate rule over the Q1 2026 column; a white rule above the Segment Adjusted EBITDA row and a white rule below it, each split at the same gap. No highlighted cells. One logo: a small white stylised X-with-swoosh mark at bottom-left. No photographs or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 fill covering the whole slide. Plain black background with no gradient or pattern. Title and table text are white #FFFFFF. Rules are white #FFFFFF, about 0.2% of slide height thick, square-ended, no shadows. Title is a regular-weight geometric grotesque sans-serif (close to GSSans) in all caps with slight letter-spacing, cap height about 5.7% of slide height (font size about 8%). Section label 'AI' is bold sans-serif, font size about 4%. Table text is medium-weight sans-serif about 2.8% of slide height; the header and total rows are bold. Row label '(in billions)' is italic. Footnote is about 0.9% of slide height in regular sans-serif. Page number is about 1.1% in regular sans-serif. No corner radii or shadows.

**Layout**

A single full-bleed image fills the slide with no title, body text, footer or slide number visible. The white SpaceX logo sits centred horizontally in the upper-middle of the slide, its wordmark spanning about 31.7% to 61.8% of the width and its swoosh rising to the right, and the planetary horizon runs across the lower half.

**Visuals**

One photograph, full-bleed: a curved horizon of a reddish-orange planet, seen from orbit against black space. The limb is a thin blue-grey atmospheric glow running from the lower left to the right edge, rising at the centre to about 60% of the slide height. The lit reddish-brown surface sits on the left, with darker shadowed terrain fading to near-black on the right and bottom, and faint dune or crater texture is visible. The lighting is low and from the left. The composition is landscape with the horizon in the lower 40% and the black sky occupying the top. The image is not cropped, is not boxed, and has no overlay or tint. Dominant colours are black #000000, rust orange #8B4A2B and dark brown #2A1B14, with a blue-grey limb #3C5A6E. One logo: the white SpaceX wordmark 'SPACEX' in a squared, widely spaced extended sans-serif, followed by the white swoosh (a sweeping tapered curve crossing the final X) rising from the baseline to the upper right.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 with a full-bleed photograph of a planetary horizon covering the lower half. Black #000000 background throughout, with the full-bleed planetary photograph covering the lower half. The logo is white #FFFFFF. There are no titles or body text; the only visible text is the white logo. The logo wordmark is a custom geometric extended sans-serif with squared letterforms, about 4.9% of slide height tall. Hidden text in the file is set in GSSans-Regular at 1.49% of slide height in black #000000, which is invisible against the black background. No shapes, borders, corner radii, shadows or gradients are visible other than the photograph's own gradient falloff.
